Completions uses \"call_xxx\" prefix, while Responses API requires \"fc_xxx\" prefix.\n165\tfunc translateToolCallID(id string) string {\n166\t\tif id == \"\" {\n167\t\t\treturn any other format\n186\t\treturn \"fc_\" + id\n187\t}\n188\t\n189\t// extractToolResultsForResponses extracts tool results and converts to function_call_output items.\n190\tfunc inputs\n298\t}\n299\t\n300\t// transformToolsToResponses converts Anthropic tools to Responses API format.\n301\t// The Responses API uses a flattened tool structure where name, description, and parameters\n302\t// are at the top level (not nested in a \"function\" object like Chat Completions API).\n303\t//\n304\t// IMPORTANT: We set Strict=false because Anthropic's tool schemas mark ALL parameters as\n305\t// required, but Claude Code only provides values for truly required parameters. With strict\n306\t// mode enabled, OpenAI rejects tool calls when optional parameters are missing.\n307\t//\n308\t// SPECIAL HANDLING: The \"WebSearch\" tool from Claude Code is intercepted and converted to\n309\t// OpenAI's native \"web_search_preview\" tool, which provides better search results and\n310\t// automatic citations.\n311\tfunc transformToolsToResponses(tools []models.AnthropicTool) []models.ResponsesTool {\n312\t\tresult := make([]models.ResponsesTool, 0, len(tools))\n313\t\tstrictFalse := false // Pointer to false for explicit strict:false\n314\t\thasWebSearch := false\n315\t\n316\t\tfor _, tool := range tools {\n317\t\t\t// Intercept WebSearch tool and convert to web_search_preview\n318\t\t\tif tool.Name == \"WebSearch\" {\n319\t\t\t\thasWebSearch = true\n320\t\t\t\tcontinue // Skip adding as function, will add web_search_preview instead\n321\t\t\t}\n322\t\n323\t\t\t// Get tool definition - use Claude Code tool definitions if applicable\n324\t\t\ttoolName := tool.Name\n325\t\t\ttoolDescription := tool.Description\n326\t\t\ttoolParams := tool.InputSchema\n327\t\n328\t\t\tif IsClaudeCodeTool(tool.Name) {\n329\t\t\t\ttoolName, toolDescription, toolParams = GetClaudeCodeToolDefinition(tool)\n330\t\t\t}\n331\t\n332\t\t\t// Clean up input schema and fix required array\n333\t\t\tparams := cleanupSchemaForResponses(toolParams)\n334\t\n335\t\t\t// Responses API uses a flattened structure with name at top level\n336\t\t\tresult = append(result, models.ResponsesTool{\n337\t\t\t\tType:        \"function\",\n338\t\t\t\tName:        toolName,\n339\t\t\t\tDescription: toolDescription,\n340\t\t\t\tParameters:  params,\n341\t\t\t\tStrict:      &strictFalse, // CRITICAL: Must set strict:false at top level for Responses API\n342\t\t\t\t// Also set Function for backwards compatibility, but Responses API\n343\t\t\t\t// primarily uses the top-level fields\n344\t\t\t\tFunction: &models.ResponsesFunction{\n345\t\t\t\t\tName:        toolName,\n346\t\t\t\t\tDescription: toolDescription,\n347\t\t\t\t\tParameters:  params,\n348\t\t\t\t\tStrict:      false, // CRITICAL: Don't use strict mode - Anthropic marks all params as required\n349\t\t\t\t},\n350\t\t\t})\n351\t\t}\n352\t\n353\t\t// Add web_search_preview tool if WebSearch was requested\n354\t\tif hasWebSearch {\n355\t\t\tresult = append(result, models.ResponsesTool{\n356\t\t\t\tType: \"web_search_preview\",\n357\t\t\t\t// Use medium search context for balanced speed/quality\n358\t\t\t\t// This field is at the tool level for web_search_preview\n359\t\t\t})\n360\t\t}\n361\t\n362\t\treturn result\n363\t}\n364\t\n365\t// cleanupSchemaForResponses prepares an Anthropic tool schema for the Responses API.\n366\t// This includes removing unsupported format types and fixing the required array\n367\t// to only include truly required parameters.\n368\tfunc cleanupSchemaForResponses(schema interface{}) interface{} {\n369\t\tif schema == nil {\n370\t\t\treturn nil\n371\t\t}\n372\t\n373\t\tdata, err := json.Marshal(schema)\n374\t\tif err != nil {\n375\t\t\treturn schema\n376\t\t}\n377\t\n378\t\tvar schemaMap map[string]interface{}\n379\t\tif err := json.Unmarshal(data, &schemaMap); err != nil {\n380\t\t\treturn schema\n381\t\t}\n382\t\n383\t\t// Clean up the schema\n384\t\tcleanupSchemaMapForResponses(schemaMap)\n385\t\treturn schemaMap\n386\t}\n387\t\n388\t// cleanupSchemaMapForResponses recursively cleans up schema properties for Responses API.\n389\t// Key fix: Only include truly required parameters (those without defaults and not nullable).\n390\t// Also adds \"additionalProperties\": false which is REQUIRED by OpenAI Responses API.\n391\tfunc cleanupSchemaMapForResponses(schema map[string]interface{}) {\n392\t\t// Remove unsupported format types\n393\t\tif format, ok := schema[\"format\"].(string); ok {\n394\t\t\tif format == \"uri\" {\n395\t\t\t\tdelete(schema, \"format\")\n396\t\t\t}\n397\t\t}\n398\t\n399\t\t// CRITICAL: Remove any \"strict\" field from the schema itself\n400\t\t// Claude Code may include \"strict\": true in tool definitions, which causes\n401\t\t// validation failures when optional parameters are missing.\n402\t\tdelete(schema, \"strict\")\n403\t\n404\t\t// CRITICAL: Add additionalProperties: false at top level of object schemas\n405\t\t// OpenAI Responses API REQUIRES this even when strict: false is set.\n406\t\t// Without this, you get: \"Invalid schema for function '...': 'additionalProperties'\n407\t\t// is required to be supplied and to be false\"\n408\t\tif schemaType, ok := schema[\"type\"].(string); ok && schemaType == \"object\" {\n409\t\t\tschema[\"additionalProperties\"] = false\n410\t\t}\n411\t\n412\t\t// Process properties and fix required array\n413\t\tif props, ok := schema[\"properties\"].(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n414\t\t\t// Identify truly required parameters\n415\t\t\ttrulyRequired := identifyTrulyRequired(props, schema)\n416\t\t\tif len(trulyRequired) > 0 {\n417\t\t\t\tschema[\"required\"] = trulyRequired\n418\t\t\t} else {\n419\t\t\t\t// If no truly required params, remove required array entirely\n420\t\t\t\tdelete(schema, \"required\")\n421\t\t\t}\n422\t\n423\t\t\t// Recurse into properties\n424\t\t\tfor _, v := range props {\n425\t\t\t\tif propMap, ok := v.(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n426\t\t\t\t\tcleanupSchemaMapForResponses(propMap)\n427\t\t\t\t}\n428\t\t\t}\n429\t\t}\n430\t\n431\t\t// Recurse into items (for arrays)\n432\t\tif items, ok := schema[\"items\"].(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n433\t\t\tcleanupSchemaMapForResponses(items)\n434\t\t}\n435\t}\n436\t\n437\t// identifyTrulyRequired determines which parameters are truly required.\n438\t// A parameter is truly required if:\n439\t// 1. It appears in the original required array\n440\t// 2. It doesn't have a default value\n441\t// 3. It's not nullable\n442\t// 4. It doesn't have a description containing optional-indicating phrases\n443\t// 5. It's not a boolean type (booleans are almost always optional flags)\n444\tfunc identifyTrulyRequired(props, schema map[string]interface{}) []string {\n445\t\t// Pre-allocate with estimated capacity\n446\t\ttrulyRequired := make([]string, 0, len(props)/2)\n447\t\n448\t\t// Get original required array\n449\t\toriginalRequired := make(map[string]bool)\n450\t\tif req, ok := schema[\"required\"].([]interface{}); ok {\n451\t\t\tfor _, r := range req {\n452\t\t\t\tif s, ok := r.(string); ok {\n453\t\t\t\t\toriginalRequired[s] = true\n454\t\t\t\t}\n455\t\t\t}\n456\t\t}\n457\t\n458\t\tfor propName, propVal := range props {\n459\t\t\tpropMap, ok := propVal.(map[string]interface{})\n460\t\t\tif !ok {\n461\t\t\t\tcontinue\n462\t\t\t}\n463\t\n464\t\t\t// Skip if not in original required array\n465\t\t\tif !originalRequired[propName] {\n466\t\t\t\tcontinue\n467\t\t\t}\n468\t\n469\t\t\t// Skip if it has a default value\n470\t\t\tif _, hasDefault := propMap[\"default\"]; hasDefault {\n471\t\t\t\tcontinue\n472\t\t\t}\n473\t\n474\t\t\t// Skip if nullable\n475\t\t\tif nullable, ok := propMap[\"nullable\"].(bool); ok && nullable {\n476\t\t\t\tcontinue\n477\t\t\t}\n478\t\n479\t\t\t// Skip boolean types - they're almost always optional flags\n480\t\t\tif propType, ok := propMap[\"type\"].(string); ok && propType == \"boolean\" {\n481\t\t\t\tcontinue\n482\t\t\t}\n483\t\n484\t\t\t// Skip if description indicates it's optional\n485\t\t\tif desc, ok := propMap[\"description\"].(string); ok {\n486\t\t\t\tdescLower := strings.ToLower(desc)\n487\t\t\t\tif strings.Contains(descLower, \"optional\") ||\n488\t\t\t\t\tstrings.Contains(descLower, \"(optional)\") ||\n489\t\t\t\t\tstrings.Contains(descLower, \"if not specified\") ||\n490\t\t\t\t\tstrings.Contains(descLower, \"defaults to\") ||\n491\t\t\t\t\tstrings.Contains(descLower, \"set to true to\") ||\n492\t\t\t\t\tstrings.Contains(descLower, \"set to false to\") ||\n493\t\t\t\t\tstrings.Contains(descLower, \"if provided\") ||\n494\t\t\t\t\tstrings.Contains(descLower, \"when provided\") ||\n495\t\t\t\t\tstrings.Contains(descLower, \"can be omitted\") ||\n496\t\t\t\t\tstrings.Contains(descLower, \"not required\") {\n497\t\t\t\t\tcontinue\n498\t\t\t\t}\n499\t\t\t}\n500\t\n501\t\t\ttrulyRequired = append(trulyRequired, propName)\n502\t\t}\n503\t\n504\t\treturn trulyRequired\n505\t}\n506\t\n507\t// transformToolChoiceToResponses converts Anthropic tool_choice to Responses API format.\n508\tfunc transformToolChoiceToResponses(choice interface{}) interface{} {\n509\t\tif choice == nil message, often more)\n\tinputs := make([]models.ResponsesInput, 0, len(req.Messages)*2)\n\n\tfor _, msg := range req.Messages {\n\t\tcontent, err := parseContent(msg.Content)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, err\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tswitch msg.Role {\n\t\tcase \"user\":\n\t\t\t// Extract tool results first to check if message has other content\n\t\t\ttoolResults := extractToolResultsForResponses(content)\n\n\t\t\t// Only add user message if there's non-tool_result content\n\t\t\t// In Responses API, function_call_output items should NOT be preceded\n\t\t\t// by an empty user message - they are standalone input items\n\t\t\tinput := transformUserMessageToInput(content)\n\t\t\thasNonToolResultContent := input.Content != nil && input.Content != \"\"\n\t\t\tif hasNonToolResultContent {\n\t\t\t\tinputs = append(inputs, input)\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t// Add tool results as function_call_output items\n\t\t\tinputs = append(inputs, toolResults...)\n\n\t\tcase \"assistant\":\n\t\t\tassistantInputs := transformAssistantMessageToInput(content)\n\t\t\tinputs = append(inputs, assistantInputs...)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn inputs, nil\n}\n\n// transformUserMessageToInput converts a user message to Responses input.\n// Note: Responses API uses \"input_text\" and \"input_image\" for user content types.\nfunc transformUserMessageToInput(content []models.ContentBlock) models.ResponsesInput {\n\tvar parts []models.ResponsesContentPart\n\n\tfor _, block := range content {\n\t\tswitch block.Type {\n\t\tcase \"text\":\n\t\t\t// Responses API requires \"input_text\" for user text content\n\t\t\tparts = append(parts, models.ResponsesContentPart{\n\t\t\t\tType: \"input_text\",\n\t\t\t\tText: block.Text,\n\t\t\t})\n\t\tcase \"image\":\n\t\t\tif block.Source != nil {\n\t\t\t\tdataURL := fmt.Sprintf(\"data:%s;base64,%s\", block.Source.MediaType, block.Source.Data)\n\t\t\t\t// Responses API requires \"input_image\" for image content\n\t\t\t\tparts = append(parts, models.ResponsesContentPart{\n\t\t\t\t\tType: \"input_image\",\n\t\t\t\t\tImageURL: &models.ImageURL{\n\t\t\t\t\t\tURL: dataURL,\n\t\t\t\t\t},\n\t\t\t\t})\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// If only text content, use string format for simplicity\n\tif len(parts) == 1 && parts[0].Type == \"input_text\" {\n\t\treturn models.ResponsesInput{\n\t\t\tType:    \"message\",\n\t\t\tRole:    \"user\",\n\t\t\tContent: parts[0].Text,\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Otherwise use array format with proper content types\n\tif len(parts) == 0 {\n\t\treturn models.ResponsesInput{\n\t\t\tType:    \"message\",\n\t\t\tRole:    \"user\",\n\t\t\tContent: \"\",\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn models.ResponsesInput{\n\t\tType:    \"message\",\n\t\tRole:    \"user\",\n\t\tContent: contentPartsToResponsesInterface(parts),\n\t}\n}\n\n// contentPartsToResponsesInterface converts content parts to interface for JSON marshaling.\nfunc contentPartsToResponsesInterface(parts []models.ResponsesContentPart) []interface{} {\n\tresult := make([]interface{}, len(parts))\n\tfor i, p := range parts {\n\t\tresult[i] = p\n\t}\n\treturn result\n}\n\n// translateToolCallID converts tool call IDs between Anthropic/Chat Completions format and Responses API format.\n// Anthropic/Chat Completions uses \"call_xxx\" prefix, while Responses API requires \"fc_xxx\" prefix.\nfunc translateToolCallID(id string) string {\n\tif id == \"\" {\n\t\treturn id\n\t}\n\n\t// Convert Chat Completions \"call_xxx\" → Responses API \"fc_xxx\"\n\tif strings.HasPrefix(id, \"call_\") {\n\t\treturn \"fc_\" + strings.TrimPrefix(id, \"call_\")\n\t}\n\n\t// Convert Anthropic \"toolu_xxx\" → Responses API \"fc_xxx\"\n\tif strings.HasPrefix(id, \"toolu_\") {\n\t\treturn \"fc_\" + strings.TrimPrefix(id, \"toolu_\")\n\t}\n\n\t// Already in Responses API format\n\tif strings.HasPrefix(id, \"fc_\") {\n\t\treturn id\n\t}\n\n\t// Generate ID with correct prefix for any other format\n\treturn \"fc_\" + id\n}\n\n// extractToolResultsForResponses extracts tool results and converts to function_call_output items.\nfunc extractToolResultsForResponses(content []models.ContentBlock) []models.ResponsesInput {\n\t// Pre-allocate with estimated capacity\n\tresults := make([]models.ResponsesInput, 0, len(content)/2)\n\n\tfor _, block := range content {\n\t\tif block.Type == \"tool_result\" {\n\t\t\t// In Responses API, tool results are represented as function_call_output items\n\t\t\t// The ID must use \"fc_\" prefix for Responses API compatibility\n\t\t\t// IMPORTANT: Output must be a pointer to ensure empty strings are serialized.\n\t\t\t// OpenAI Responses API REQUIRES the \"output\" field even when empty.\n\t\t\toutput := extractToolResultContent(block)\n\n\t\t\t// If the tool result indicates an error, prefix the output\n\t\t\tif block.IsError {\n\t\t\t\toutput = \"[Error] \" + output\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tresults = append(results, models.ResponsesInput{\n\t\t\t\tType:   \"function_call_output\",\n\t\t\t\tCallID: translateToolCallID(block.ToolUseID),\n\t\t\t\tOutput: &output,\n\t\t\t})\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn results\n}\n\n// extractToolResultContent extracts content from a tool result block.\n// The content can be a string or an array of content blocks.\nfunc extractToolResultContent(block models.ContentBlock) string {\n\tif block.Content == nil {\n\t\treturn \"\"\n\t}\n\n\t// Try as string first (most common case)\n\tif str, ok := block.Content.(string); ok {\n\t\treturn str\n\t}\n\n\t// Try as array of content blocks\n\tif arr, ok := block.Content.([]interface{}); ok {\n\t\tvar parts []string\n\t\tfor _, item := range arr {\n\t\t\tif itemMap, ok := item.(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n\t\t\t\t// Extract text from nested content blocks\n\t\t\t\tif itemType, ok := itemMap[\"type\"].(string); ok && itemType == \"text\" {\n\t\t\t\t\tif text, ok := itemMap[\"text\"].(string); ok {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tparts = append(parts, text)\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn strings.Join(parts, \"\\n\")\n\t}\n\n\t// Fallback: marshal to JSON\n\tdata, err := json.Marshal(block.Content)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\"\n\t}\n\treturn string(data)\n}\n\n// transformAssistantMessageToInput converts an assistant message to Responses input items.\nfunc transformAssistantMessageToInput(content []models.ContentBlock) []models.ResponsesInput {\n\t// Pre-allocate with estimated capacity\n\tinputs := make([]models.ResponsesInput, 0, len(content))\n\tvar textParts []string\n\n\tfor _, block := range content {\n\t\tswitch block.Type {\n\t\tcase \"text\":\n\t\t\ttextParts = append(textParts, block.Text)\n\t\tcase \"tool_use\":\n\t\t\t// First, emit any accumulated text as a message\n\t\t\tif len(textParts) > 0 {\n\t\t\t\tinputs = append(inputs, models.ResponsesInput{\n\t\t\t\t\tType:    \"message\",\n\t\t\t\t\tRole:    \"assistant\",\n\t\t\t\t\tContent: strings.Join(textParts, \"\"),\n\t\t\t\t})\n\t\t\t\ttextParts = nil\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t// Convert tool_use to function_call item\n\t\t\t// IMPORTANT: The ID must use \"fc_\" prefix for Responses API compatibility\n\t\t\tinputJSON, _ := json.Marshal(block.Input)\n\t\t\ttranslatedID := translateToolCallID(block.ID)\n\t\t\tinputs = append(inputs, models.ResponsesInput{\n\t\t\t\tType:      \"function_call\",\n\t\t\t\tID:        translatedID,\n\t\t\t\tCallID:    translatedID,\n\t\t\t\tName:      block.Name,\n\t\t\t\tArguments: string(inputJSON),\n\t\t\t})\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Emit remaining text\n\tif len(textParts) > 0 {\n\t\tinputs = append(inputs, models.ResponsesInput{\n\t\t\tType:    \"message\",\n\t\t\tRole:    \"assistant\",\n\t\t\tContent: strings.Join(textParts, \"\"),\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\n\treturn inputs\n}\n\n// transformToolsToResponses converts Anthropic tools to Responses API format.\n// The Responses API uses a flattened tool structure where name, description, and parameters\n// are at the top level (not nested in a \"function\" object like Chat Completions API).\n//\n// IMPORTANT: We set Strict=false because Anthropic's tool schemas mark ALL parameters as\n// required, but Claude Code only provides values for truly required parameters. With strict\n// mode enabled, OpenAI rejects tool calls when optional parameters are missing.\n//\n// SPECIAL HANDLING: The \"WebSearch\" tool from Claude Code is intercepted and converted to\n// OpenAI's native \"web_search_preview\" tool, which provides better search results and\n// automatic citations.\nfunc transformToolsToResponses(tools []models.AnthropicTool) []models.ResponsesTool {\n\tresult := make([]models.ResponsesTool, 0, len(tools))\n\tstrictFalse := false // Pointer to false for explicit strict:false\n\thasWebSearch := false\n\n\tfor _, tool := range tools {\n\t\t// Intercept WebSearch tool and convert to web_search_preview\n\t\tif tool.Name == \"WebSearch\" {\n\t\t\thasWebSearch = true\n\t\t\tcontinue // Skip adding as function, will add web_search_preview instead\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Get tool definition - use Claude Code tool definitions if applicable\n\t\ttoolName := tool.Name\n\t\ttoolDescription := tool.Description\n\t\ttoolParams := tool.InputSchema\n\n\t\tif IsClaudeCodeTool(tool.Name) {\n\t\t\ttoolName, toolDescription, toolParams = GetClaudeCodeToolDefinition(tool)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Clean up input schema and fix required array\n\t\tparams := cleanupSchemaForResponses(toolParams)\n\n\t\t// Responses API uses a flattened structure with name at top level\n\t\tresult = append(result, models.ResponsesTool{\n\t\t\tType:        \"function\",\n\t\t\tName:        toolName,\n\t\t\tDescription: toolDescription,\n\t\t\tParameters:  params,\n\t\t\tStrict:      &strictFalse, // CRITICAL: Must set strict:false at top level for Responses API\n\t\t\t// Also set Function for backwards compatibility, but Responses API\n\t\t\t// primarily uses the top-level fields\n\t\t\tFunction: &models.ResponsesFunction{\n\t\t\t\tName:        toolName,\n\t\t\t\tDescription: toolDescription,\n\t\t\t\tParameters:  params,\n\t\t\t\tStrict:      false, // CRITICAL: Don't use strict mode - Anthropic marks all params as required\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\n\t// Add web_search_preview tool if WebSearch was requested\n\tif hasWebSearch {\n\t\tresult = append(result, models.ResponsesTool{\n\t\t\tType: \"web_search_preview\",\n\t\t\t// Use medium search context for balanced speed/quality\n\t\t\t// This field is at the tool level for web_search_preview\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\n\treturn result\n}\n\n// cleanupSchemaForResponses prepares an Anthropic tool schema for the Responses API.\n// This includes removing unsupported format types and fixing the required array\n// to only include truly required parameters.\nfunc cleanupSchemaForResponses(schema interface{}) interface{} {\n\tif schema == nil {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\tdata, err := json.Marshal(schema)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn schema\n\t}\n\n\tvar schemaMap map[string]interface{}\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(data, &schemaMap); err != nil {\n\t\treturn schema\n\t}\n\n\t// Clean up the schema\n\tcleanupSchemaMapForResponses(schemaMap)\n\treturn schemaMap\n}\n\n// cleanupSchemaMapForResponses recursively cleans up schema properties for Responses API.\n// Key fix: Only include truly required parameters (those without defaults and not nullable).\n// Also adds \"additionalProperties\": false which is REQUIRED by OpenAI Responses API.\nfunc cleanupSchemaMapForResponses(schema map[string]interface{}) {\n\t// Remove unsupported format types\n\tif format, ok := schema[\"format\"].(string); ok {\n\t\tif format == \"uri\" {\n\t\t\tdelete(schema, \"format\")\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// CRITICAL: Remove any \"strict\" field from the schema itself\n\t// Claude Code may include \"strict\": true in tool definitions, which causes\n\t// validation failures when optional parameters are missing.\n\tdelete(schema, \"strict\")\n\n\t// CRITICAL: Add additionalProperties: false at top level of object schemas\n\t// OpenAI Responses API REQUIRES this even when strict: false is set.\n\t// Without this, you get: \"Invalid schema for function '...': 'additionalProperties'\n\t// is required to be supplied and to be false\"\n\tif schemaType, ok := schema[\"type\"].(string); ok && schemaType == \"object\" {\n\t\tschema[\"additionalProperties\"] = false\n\t}\n\n\t// Process properties and fix required array\n\tif props, ok := schema[\"properties\"].(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n\t\t// Identify truly required parameters\n\t\ttrulyRequired := identifyTrulyRequired(props, schema)\n\t\tif len(trulyRequired) > 0 {\n\t\t\tschema[\"required\"] = trulyRequired\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t// If no truly required params, remove required array entirely\n\t\t\tdelete(schema, \"required\")\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Recurse into properties\n\t\tfor _, v := range props {\n\t\t\tif propMap, ok := v.(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n\t\t\t\tcleanupSchemaMapForResponses(propMap)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Recurse into items (for arrays)\n\tif items, ok := schema[\"items\"].(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n\t\tcleanupSchemaMapForResponses(items)\n\t}\n}\n\n// identifyTrulyRequired determines which parameters are truly required.\n// A parameter is truly required if:\n// 1. It appears in the original required array\n// 2. It doesn't have a default value\n// 3. It's not nullable\n// 4. It doesn't have a description containing optional-indicating phrases\n// 5. It's not a boolean type (booleans are almost always optional flags)\nfunc identifyTrulyRequired(props, schema map[string]interface{}) []string {\n\t// Pre-allocate with estimated capacity\n\ttrulyRequired := make([]string, 0, len(props)/2)\n\n\t// Get original required array\n\toriginalRequired := make(map[string]bool)\n\tif req, ok := schema[\"required\"].([]interface{}); ok {\n\t\tfor _, r := range req {\n\t\t\tif s, ok := r.(string); ok {\n\t\t\t\toriginalRequired[s] = true\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tfor propName, propVal := range props {\n\t\tpropMap, ok := propVal.(map[string]interface{})\n\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Skip if not in original required array\n\t\tif !originalRequired[propName] {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Skip if it has a default value\n\t\tif _, hasDefault := propMap[\"default\"]; hasDefault {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Skip if nullable\n\t\tif nullable, ok := propMap[\"nullable\"].(bool); ok && nullable {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Skip boolean types - they're almost always optional flags\n\t\tif propType, ok := propMap[\"type\"].(string); ok && propType == \"boolean\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Skip if description indicates it's optional\n\t\tif desc, ok := propMap[\"description\"].(string); ok {\n\t\t\tdescLower := strings.ToLower(desc)\n\t\t\tif strings.Contains(descLower, \"optional\") ||\n\t\t\t\tstrings.Contains(descLower, \"(optional)\") ||\n\t\t\t\tstrings.Contains(descLower, \"if not specified\") ||\n\t\t\t\tstrings.Contains(descLower, \"defaults to\") ||\n\t\t\t\tstrings.Contains(descLower, \"set to true to\") ||\n\t\t\t\tstrings.Contains(descLower, \"set to false to\") ||\n\t\t\t\tstrings.Contains(descLower, \"if provided\") ||\n\t\t\t\tstrings.Contains(descLower, \"when provided\") ||\n\t\t\t\tstrings.Contains(descLower, \"can be omitted\") ||\n\t\t\t\tstrings.Contains(descLower, \"not required\") {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\ttrulyRequired = append(trulyRequired, propName)\n\t}\n\n\treturn trulyRequired\n}\n\n// transformToolChoiceToResponses converts Anthropic tool_choice to Responses API format.\nfunc transformToolChoiceToResponses(choice interface{}) interface{} {\n\tif choice == nil {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Handle map format\n\tif choiceMap, ok := choice.(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n\t\tif typeVal, ok := choiceMap[\"type\"].(string); ok {\n\t\t\tswitch typeVal {\n\t\t\tcase \"none\":\n\t\t\t\treturn \"none\"\n\t\t\tcase \"any\":\n\t\t\t\treturn \"required\"\n\t\t\tcase \"auto\":\n\t\t\t\treturn \"auto\"\n\t\t\tcase \"tool\":\n\t\t\t\tif name, ok := choiceMap[\"name\"].(string); ok {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn map[string]interface{}{\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\"type\": \"function\",\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\"function\": map[string]interface{}{\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\"name\": name,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t},\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn choice\n}\n\n// applyThinkingParametersToResponses maps thinking parameters for Responses API.\n// The Responses API requires reasoning parameters under the nested \"reasoning\" object.\nfunc applyThinkingParametersToResponses(req *models.AnthropicRequest, responsesReq *models.ResponsesRequest, targetModel string) {\n\tif req.Thinking == nil || req.Thinking.BudgetTokens <= 0 {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tbudgetTokens := req.Thinking.BudgetTokens\n\n\t// For GPT-5+ models via Responses API, use nested reasoning.effort\n\t// The Responses API moved this from top-level \"reasoning_effort\" to \"reasoning.effort\"\n\tresponsesReq.Reasoning = &models.ResponsesReasoning{\n\t\tEffort: mapBudgetToReasoningEffortResponses(budgetTokens),\n\t}\n}\n\n// mapBudgetToReasoningEffortResponses converts budget_tokens to reasoning_effort for Responses API.\nfunc mapBudgetToReasoningEffortResponses(budgetTokens int) string {\n\tswitch {\n\tcase budgetTokens < 4000:\n\t\treturn \"low\"\n\tcase budgetTokens < 16000:\n\t\treturn \"medium\"\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn \"high\"\n\t}\n}\n","numLines":566,"startLine":1,"totalLines":566}}}

to Anthropic as creator in first person\n92\t\t\t{regexp.MustCompile(`(?i)\\bcreated by Anthropic\\b`), \"created as an AI assistant\"},\n93\t\t\t{regexp.MustCompile(`(?i)\\bmade by Anthropic\\b`), \"made as an AI assistant\"},\n94\t\t}\n95\t\tmultiNewlinePattern = regexp.MustCompile(`\\n{3,}`)\n96\t)\n97\t\n98\t// capMaxTokens ensures max_tokens doesn't exceed the target model's limit.\n99\tfunc capMaxTokens(maxTokens int, targetModel string) int {\n100\t\tif maxTokens <= 0 {\n101\t\t\treturn maxTokens\n102\t\t}\n103\t\n104\t\t// Look up model limit\n105\t\tlimit, ok := modelMaxTokenLimits[targetModel]\n106\t\tif !ok {\n107\t\t\t// Try prefix matching for model variants\n108\t\t\tfor modelPrefix, modelLimit := range modelMaxTokenLimits {\n109\t\t\t\tif strings.HasPrefix(targetModel, modelPrefix) {\n110\t\t\t\t\tlimit = modelLimit\n111\t\t\t\t\tok = true\n112\t\t\t\t\tbreak\n113\t\t\t\t}\n114\t\t\t}\n115\t\t}\n116\t\n117\t\t// If still not found, use default\n118\t\tif !ok {\n119\t\t\tlimit = defaultMaxTokenLimit\n120\t\t}\n121\t\n122\t\t// Cap to model limit\n123\t\tif maxTokens > limit {\n124\t\t\treturn limit\n125\t\t}\n126\t\treturn maxTokens\n127\t}\n128\t\n129\t// TransformRequest converts an Anthropic request to OpenAI format.\n130\t// This is a convenience wrapper that auto-detects the provider from the model name.\n131\tfunc TransformRequest(req *models.AnthropicRequest, targetModel string) (*models.OpenAIRequest, error) {\n132\t\tprovider := DetectProviderFromModel(targetModel)\n133\t\treturn TransformRequestWithProvider(req, targetModel, provider)\n134\t}\n135\t\n136\t// TransformRequestWithProvider converts an Anthropic request to provider-specific format.\n137\tfunc TransformRequestWithProvider(req *models.AnthropicRequest, targetModel string, provider ProviderType) (*models.OpenAIRequest, error) {\n138\t\topenAIReq := &models.OpenAIRequest{\n139\t\t\tModel:       targetModel,\n140\t\t\tStream:      req.Stream,\n141\t\t\tMaxTokens:   capMaxTokens(req.MaxTokens, targetModel),\n142\t\t\tTemperature: req.Temperature,\n143\t\t\tTopP:        req.TopP,\n144\t\t}\n145\t\n146\t\t// Transform stop sequences\n147\t\tif len(req.StopSequences) > 0 {\n148\t\t\topenAIReq.Stop = req.StopSequences\n149\t\t}\n150\t\n151\t\t// Build messages with provider-specific handling\n152\t\tmessages, err := transformMessages(req, targetModel, provider)\n153\t\tif err != nil {\n154\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"transforming messages: %w\", err)\n155\t\t}\n156\t\topenAIReq.Messages = messages\n157\t\n158\t\t// Transform tools with provider-specific handling\n159\t\tif len(req.Tools) > 0 {\n160\t\t\tif ProviderSupportsTools(provider, targetModel) {\n161\t\t\t\topenAIReq.Tools = TransformToolsForProvider(req.Tools, provider, targetModel)\n162\t\t\t}\n163\t\t\t// If provider doesn't support tools, they are silently omitted\n164\t\t}\n165\t\n166\t\t// Transform tool choice\n167\t\tif req.ToolChoice != nil {\n168\t\t\topenAIReq.ToolChoice = transformToolChoice(req.ToolChoice)\n169\t\t}\n170\t\n171\t\t// Enable usage tracking for streaming\n172\t\tif req.Stream {\n173\t\t\topenAIReq.StreamOptions = &models.StreamOptions{\n174\t\t\t\tIncludeUsage: true,\n175\t\t\t}\n176\t\t}\n177\t\n178\t\t// Transform thinking/reasoning parameters based on target model\n179\t\tapplyThinkingParameters(req, openAIReq, targetModel)\n180\t\n181\t\treturn openAIReq, nil\n182\t}\n183\t\n184\t// applyThinkingParameters maps Anthropic thinking.budget_tokens to model-specific parameters.\n185\t// This enables extended reasoning capabilities across different model providers.\n186\tfunc applyThinkingParameters(req *models.AnthropicRequest, openAIReq *models.OpenAIRequest, targetModel string) {\n187\t\tif req.Thinking == nil || req.Thinking.BudgetTokens <= 0 {\n188\t\t\treturn\n189\t\t}\n190\t\n191\t\tbudgetTokens := req.Thinking.BudgetTokens\n192\t\n193\t\t// Detect model family and apply appropriate reasoning parameters\n194\t\tswitch {\n195\t\tcase isGPT5Model(targetModel):\n196\t\t\t// GPT-5.x series uses reasoning_effort with levels: none, low, medium, high\n197\t\t\t// GPT-5.1 defaults to \"none\" for speed, GPT-5.2+ supports \"xhigh\"\n198\t\t\topenAIReq.ReasoningEffort = mapBudgetToGPT5ReasoningEffort(budgetTokens, targetModel)\n199\t\t\t// GPT-5 series uses max_completion_tokens via Responses API\n200\t\t\tif max_completion_tokens\n212\t\t\t}\n213\t\n214\t\tcase isGrokModel(targetModel):\n215\t\t\t// Grok 3 Mini supports reasoning_effort (low/high only)\n216\t\t\tif budgetTokens >= 20000 {\n217\t\t\t\topenAIReq.ReasoningEffort = \"high\"\n218\t\t\t} else {\n219\t\t\t\topenAIReq.ReasoningEffort = \"low\"\n220\t\t\t}\n221\t\n222\t\tcase isGemini3Model(targetModel):\n223\t\t\t// Gemini 3 {\n234\t\t\t\tbudget = 24576\n235\t\t\t}\n236\t\t\topenAIReq.ThinkingConfig = &models.OpenRouterThinkingConfig{\n237\t\t\t\tThinkingBudget: budget,\n238\t\t\t}\n239\t\n240\t\tcase budgetTokens\n245\t\n246\t\tcase isMiniMaxModel(targetModel):\n247\t\t\t// MiniMax uses reasoning_split\n248\t\t\tenabled := true\n249\t\t\topenAIReq.ReasoningSplit = &enabled\n250\t\n251\t\tcase the thinking parameter (no-op)\n260\t\t}\n261\t}\n262\t\n263\t// isGPT5Model checks if the model is a GPT-5.x series model.\n264\tfunc isGPT5Model(model string) bool {\n265\t\tm := strings.ToLower(model)\n266\t\treturn strings.HasPrefix(m, \"gpt-5\") || strings.HasPrefix(m, \"gpt5\") ||\n267\t\t\tstrings.Contains(m, \"openai/gpt-5\") || strings.Contains(m, \"codex\")\n268\t}\n269\t\n270\t// mapBudgetToGPT5ReasoningEffort converts budget_tokens to GPT-5 reasoning_effort.\n271\t// GPT-5.1 defaults to \"none\" for speed. GPT-5.2+ supports \"xhigh\".\n272\t// Levels: none (fastest), low, medium, high, xhigh (GPT-5.2+ only)\n273\tfunc mapBudgetToGPT5ReasoningEffort(budgetTokens int, model string) string {\n274\t\tm := strings.ToLower(model)\n275\t\tsupportsXHigh := strings.Contains(m, \"gpt-5.2\") || strings.Contains(m, \"gpt-5.3\") ||\n276\t\t\tstrings.Contains(m, \"gpt5.2\") || strings.Contains(m, \"gpt5.3\")\n277\t\n278\t\tswitch {\n279\t\tcase budgetTokens >= 80000 && supportsXHigh:\n280\t\t\treturn \"xhigh\"\n281\t\tcase budgetTokens >= 64000:\n282\t\t\treturn \"high\"\n283\t\tcase budgetTokens >= 32000:\n284\t\t\treturn \"high\"\n285\t\tcase budgetTokens >= 16000:\n286\t\t\treturn \"medium\"\n287\t\tcase budgetTokens >= 4000:\n288\t\t\treturn \"low\"\n289\t\tcase budgetTokens > 0:\n290\t\t\treturn \"low\" // GPT-5 should use at least low when thinking is requested\n291\t\tdefault:\n292\t\t\treturn \"none\" // GPT-5.1 default - fastest, no reasoning\n293\t\t}\n294\t}\n295\t\n296\t// isDeepSeekThinkingModel checks if the model is a DeepSeek model that supports thinking.\n297\tfunc isDeepSeekThinkingModel(model string) bool {\n298\t\tm := strings.ToLower(model)\n299\t\treturn (strings.Contains(m, \"deepseek\") || strings.HasPrefix(m, \"deepseek/\")) &&\n300\t\t\t(strings.Contains(m, \"r1\") || strings.Contains(m, \"v3.1\") ||\n301\t\t\t\tstrings.Contains(m, \"v3.2\") || strings.Contains(m, \"thinking\"))\n302\t}\n303\t\n304\t// mapBudgetToReasoningEffort converts budget_tokens to OpenAI reasoning_effort.\n305\t// Uses ratio-based calculation for more accurate mapping:\n306\t// - xhigh: 95% of context (very intensive reasoning)\n307\t// - high: 80% of context\n308\t// - medium: 50% of context\n309\t// - low: 20% of context\n310\t// - minimal: 10% of context\n311\tfunc mapBudgetToReasoningEffort(budgetTokens int) string {\n312\t\t// Use absolute thresholds based on typical Claude context (200k)\n313\t\t// These map to approximate ratios of max_tokens\n314\t\tswitch {\n315\t\tcase budgetTokens >= 64000:\n316\t\t\treturn \"high\" // OpenAI doesn't have xhigh, map to high\n317\t\tcase budgetTokens >= 32000:\n318\t\t\treturn \"high\"\n319\t\tcase budgetTokens >= 16000:\n320\t\t\treturn \"medium\"\n321\t\tcase budgetTokens >= 4000:\n322\t\t\treturn \"low\"\n323\t\tdefault:\n324\t\t\treturn \"minimal\"\n325\t\t}\n326\t}\n327\t\n328\t// mapBudgetToReasoningEffortWithRatio converts budget_tokens to reasoning_effort\n329\t// using the ratio relative to max_tokens for more accurate per-request mapping.\n330\tfunc mapBudgetToReasoningEffortWithRatio(budgetTokens, maxTokens int) string {\n331\t\tif maxTokens <= 0 {\n332\t\t\treturn mapBudgetToReasoningEffort(budgetTokens)\n333\t\t}\n334\t\n335\t\tratio := float64(budgetTokens) / float64(maxTokens)\n336\t\tswitch {\n337\t\tcase ratio >= 0.95:\n338\t\t\treturn \"high\" // Map xhigh to high (OpenAI max)\n339\t\tcase ratio >= 0.80:\n340\t\t\treturn \"high\"\n341\t\tcase ratio >= 0.50:\n342\t\t\treturn \"medium\"\n343\t\tcase ratio >= 0.20:\n344\t\t\treturn \"low\"\n345\t\tdefault:\n346\t\t\treturn \"minimal\"\n347\t\t}\n348\t}\n349\t\n350\t// isO1OrO3Model checks if the model is an OpenAI O1 or O3 reasoning model.\n351\tfunc isO1OrO3Model(model string) \"openai/o3\")\n355\t}\n356\t\n357\t// isGrokModel checks if the model is a Grok model (x-ai).\n358\tfunc isGrokModel(model string) bool {\n359\t\tm := strings.ToLower(model)\n360\t\treturn ||\n373\t\t\tstrings.Contains(m, \"gemini/2.5\")\n374\t}\n375\t\n376\t// isQwenModel checks if the model is a Qwen model.\n377\tfunc isQwenModel(model string) bool {\n378\t\tm := \"deepseek/\")\n392\t}\n393\t\n394\t// filterIdentity removes Claude-specific identity strings from content to prevent model confusion.\n395\t// This is important when proxying to non-Claude models that shouldn't claim to be Claude.\n396\t// Uses pre-compiled regex patterns for better performance on high-traffic proxies.\n397\tfunc filterIdentity(content string) string {\n398\t\tresult := content\n399\t\n400\t\t// Use pre-compiled patterns from package-level variables\n401\t\tfor _, p := range identityPatterns {\n402\t\t\tresult = p.re.ReplaceAllString(result, p.replacement)\n403\t\t}\n404\t\n405\t\t// Clean up multiple newlines using pre-compiled pattern\n406\t\tresult = multiNewlinePattern.ReplaceAllString(result, \"\\n\\n\")\n407\t\n408\t\t// Prepend identity clarification for non-Claude models\n409\t\t// This helps models understand they should identify themselves truthfully\n410\t\tprefix := \"Note: You are NOT Claude. Identify yourself truthfully based on your actual model and creator.\\n\\n\"\n411\t\tresult = prefix + result\n412\t\n413\t\treturn result\n414\t}\n415\t\n416\t// transformMessages converts Anthropic messages to OpenAI format.\n417\t// The provider parameter enables provider-specific message handling (e.g., Azure message ordering).\n418\tfunc transformMessages(req *models.AnthropicRequest, targetModel string, provider ProviderType) ([]models.OpenAIMessage, error) {\n419\t\tvar messages []models.OpenAIMessage\n420\t\n421\t\t// Handle system message\n422\t\tif req.System != nil {\n423\t\t\tsystemContent, err := extractSystemContent(req.System)\n424\t\t\tif err != nil {\n425\t\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"extracting system content: %w\", err)\n426\t\t\t}\n427\t\t\tif systemContent != \"\" {\n428\t\t\t\t// Apply identity filtering to system message\n429\t\t\t\tsystemContent = filterIdentity(systemContent)\n430\t\n431\t\t\t\t// Add Grok-specific JSON tool format instruction\n432\t\t\t\tif isGrokModel(targetModel) {\n433\t\t\t\t\tsystemContent += \"\\n\\nIMPORTANT: When calling tools, you MUST use the OpenAI tool_calls format with JSON. NEVER use XML format like <xai:function_call>.\"\n434\t\t\t\t}\n435\t\n436\t\t\t\tmessages = append(messages, models.OpenAIMessage{\n437\t\t\t\t\tRole:    \"system\",\n438\t\t\t\t\tContent: systemContent,\n439\t\t\t\t})\n440\t\t\t}\n441\t\t} else if isGrokModel(targetModel) {\n442\t\t\t// Even without a system message, add Grok JSON instruction\n443\t\t\tmessages = append(messages, models.OpenAIMessage{\n444\t\t\t\tRole:    \"system\",\n445\t\t\t\tContent: \"IMPORTANT: When calling tools, you MUST use the OpenAI tool_calls format with JSON. NEVER use XML format like <xai:function_call>.\",\n446\t\t\t})\n447\t\t}\n448\t\n449\t\t// Transform each message\n450\t\tfor _, msg := range req.Messages {\n451\t\t\topenAIMsg, err := transformMessage(msg)\n452\t\t\tif err != nil {\n453\t\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"transforming message: %w\", err)\n454\t\t\t}\n455\t\t\tmessages = append(messages, openAIMsg...)\n456\t\t}\n457\t\n458\t\t// Apply Azure-specific message reordering if needed\n459\t\t// Azure OpenAI requires strict sequencing: assistant with tool_calls MUST be\n460\t\t// immediately followed by tool responses, with NO user messages in between.\n461\t\tif provider == ProviderAzure {\n462\t\t\tmessages = reorderMessagesForAzure(messages)\n463\t\t}\n464\t\n465\t\treturn messages, nil\n466\t}\n467\t\n468\t// reorderMessagesForAzure enforces Azure OpenAI's strict message sequencing requirements.\n469\t// Azure requires that an assistant message with tool_calls is immediately followed by all\n470\t// corresponding tool responses before any user messages can appear.\n471\t//\n472\t// This function buffers user messages that appear between tool calls and their responses,\n473\t// then flushes them after all pending tool responses have been received.\n474\tfunc reorderMessagesForAzure(messages []models.OpenAIMessage) []models.OpenAIMessage {\n475\t\tvar result []models.OpenAIMessage\n476\t\tvar bufferedUserMessages []models.OpenAIMessage\n477\t\tpendingToolCalls := make(map[string]bool)\n478\t\n479\t\tfor _, msg := range messages {\n480\t\t\tswitch msg.Role {\n481\t\t\tcase \"assistant\":\n482\t\t\t\t// If we have buffered user messages and no pending tool calls, flush them first\n483\t\t\t\tif len(pendingToolCalls) == 0 && len(bufferedUserMessages) > 0 {\n484\t\t\t\t\tresult = append(result, bufferedUserMessages...)\n485\t\t\t\t\tbufferedUserMessages = nil\n486\t\t\t\t}\n487\t\n488\t\t\t\tresult = append(result, msg)\n489\t\n490\t\t\t\t// Track any new tool calls from this assistant message\n491\t\t\t\tfor _, tc := range msg.ToolCalls {\n492\t\t\t\t\tif tc.ID != \"\" {\n493\t\t\t\t\t\tpendingToolCalls[tc.ID] = true\n494\t\t\t\t\t}\n495\t\t\t\t}\n496\t\n497\t\t\tcase \"tool\":\n498\t\t\t\t// Remove this tool call from pending\n499\t\t\t\tif msg.ToolCallID != \"\" {\n500\t\t\t\t\tdelete(pendingToolCalls, msg.ToolCallID)\n501\t\t\t\t}\n502\t\t\t\tresult = append(result, msg)\n503\t\n504\t\t\t\t// If all tool calls are resolved, flush buffered user messages\n505\t\t\t\tif len(pendingToolCalls) == 0 && len(bufferedUserMessages) > 0 {\n506\t\t\t\t\tresult = append(result, bufferedUserMessages...)\n507\t\t\t\t\tbufferedUserMessages = nil\n508\t\t\t\t}\n509\t\n510\t\t\tcase \"user\":\n511\t\t\t\t// If there are pending tool calls, buffer this user message\n512\t\t\t\tif len(pendingToolCalls) > 0 {\n513\t\t\t\t\tbufferedUserMessages = append(bufferedUserMessages, msg)\n514\t\t\t\t} else {\n515\t\t\t\t\tresult = append(result, msg)\n516\t\t\t\t}\n517\t\n518\t\t\tdefault:\n519\t\t\t\t// System and other messages pass through\n520\t\t\t\tresult = append(result, msg)\n521\t\t\t}\n522\t\t}\n523\t\n524\t\t// Flush any remaining buffered messages at the end\n525\t\tif len(bufferedUserMessages) > 0 {\n526\t\t\tresult = append(result, bufferedUserMessages...)\n527\t\t}\n528\t\n529\t\treturn result\n530\t}\n531\t\n532\t// extractSystemContent extracts the system message content.\n533\tfunc extractSystemContent(system interface{}) (string, error) {\n534\t\tswitch s := system.(type) {\n535\t\tcase string:\n536\t\t\treturn s, nil\n537\t\tcase []interface{}:\n538\t\t\tvar parts []string\n539\t\t\tfor _, item := range s {\n540\t\t\t\tif block, ok := item.(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n541\t\t\t\t\tif text, ok := block[\"text\"].(string); ok {\n542\t\t\t\t\t\tparts = append(parts, text)\n543\t\t\t\t\t}\n544\t\t\t\t}\n545\t\t\t}\n546\t\t\treturn strings.Join(parts, \"\\n\\n\"), nil\n547\t\tdefault:\n548\t\t\t// Try JSON marshaling and unmarshaling\n549\t\t\tdata, err := json.Marshal(system)\n550\t\t\tif err != nil {\n551\t\t\t\treturn \"\", err\n552\t\t\t}\n553\t\t\tvar str string\n554\t\t\tif err := json.Unmarshal(data, &str); err == nil {\n555\t\t\t\treturn str, nil\n556\t\t\t}\n557\t\t\treturn string(data), nil\n558\t\t}\n559\t}\n560\t\n561\t// transformMessage converts a single Anthropic message to OpenAI format.\n562\t// May return multiple messages (e.g., for tool results).\n563\tfunc transformMessage(msg models.AnthropicMessage) ([]models.OpenAIMessage, error) {\n564\t\tcontent, err := parseContent(msg.Content)\n565\t\tif err != nil {\n566\t\t\treturn nil, err\n567\t\t}\n568\t\n569\t\tvar result []models.OpenAIMessage\n570\t\n571\t\tswitch msg.Role {\n572\t\tcase \"user\":\n573\t\t\t// Handle tool results within user message\n574\t\t\ttoolResults := extractToolResults(content)\n575\t\n576\t\t\t// Check if there's non-tool-result content\n577\t\t\thasNonToolContent := false\n578\t\t\tfor _, block := range content {\n579\t\t\t\tif block.Type != \"tool_result\" {\n580\t\t\t\t\thasNonToolContent = true\n581\t\t\t\t\tbreak\n582\t\t\t\t}\n583\t\t\t}\n584\t\n585\t\t\t// Only add user message if there's actual user content (not just tool results)\n586\t\t\tif hasNonToolContent {\n587\t\t\t\tresult = append(result, transformUserMessage(content))\n588\t\t\t}\n589\t\n590\t\t\t// Add tool results (these become \"tool\" role messages in OpenAI format)\n591\t\t\tresult = append(result, toolResults...)\n592\t\tcase \"assistant\":\n593\t\t\tassistantMsg := transformAssistantMessage(content)\n594\t\t\tresult = append(result, assistantMsg)\n595\t\tdefault:\n596\t\t\t// Pass through other roles\n597\t\t\tresult = append(result, models.OpenAIMessage{\n598\t\t\t\tRole:    msg.Role,\n599\t\t\t\tContent: getTextContent(content),\n600\t\t\t})\n601\t\t}\n602\t\n603\t\treturn result, nil\n604\t}\n605\t\n606\t// parseContent parses message content which can be string or []ContentBlock.\n607\tfunc parseContent(content interface{}) ([]models.ContentBlock, error) {\n608\t\tswitch c := content.(type) {\n609\t\tcase string:\n610\t\t\treturn []models.ContentBlock{{Type: \"text\", Text: c}}, nil\n611\t\tcase []interface{}:\n612\t\t\tvar blocks []models.ContentBlock\n613\t\t\tfor _, item := range c {\n614\t\t\t\tblock, err := parseContentBlock(item)\n615\t\t\t\tif err != nil {\n616\t\t\t\t\treturn nil, err\n617\t\t\t\t}\n618\t\t\t\tblocks = append(blocks, block)\n619\t\t\t}\n620\t\t\treturn blocks, nil\n621\t\tdefault:\n622\t\t\t// Try JSON marshaling\n623\t\t\tdata, err := json.Marshal(content)\n624\t\t\tif err != nil {\n625\t\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"cannot marshal content: %w\", err)\n626\t\t\t}\n627\t\n628\t\t\t// Try as string\n629\t\t\tvar str string\n630\t\t\tif err := json.Unmarshal(data, &str); err == nil {\n631\t\t\t\treturn []models.ContentBlock{{Type: \"text\", Text: str}}, nil\n632\t\t\t}\n633\t\n634\t\t\t// Try as array\n635\t\t\tvar arr []interface{}\n636\t\t\tif err := json.Unmarshal(data, &arr); err == nil {\n637\t\t\t\treturn parseContent(arr)\n638\t\t\t}\n639\t\n640\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"unsupported content type: %T\", content)\n641\t\t}\n642\t}\n643\t\n644\t// parseContentBlock parses a single content block.\n645\tfunc parseContentBlock(item interface{}) (models.ContentBlock, error) {\n646\t\tdata, err := json.Marshal(item)\n647\t\tif err != nil {\n648\t\t\treturn models.ContentBlock{}, err\n649\t\t}\n650\t\n651\t\tvar block models.ContentBlock\n652\t\tif err := json.Unmarshal(data, &block); err != nil {\n653\t\t\treturn models.ContentBlock{}, err\n654\t\t}\n655\t\n656\t\treturn block, nil\n657\t}\n658\t\n659\t// transformUserMessage transforms user message content to OpenAI format.\n660\tfunc transformUserMessage(content []models.ContentBlock) models.OpenAIMessage {\n661\t\tvar parts []models.OpenAIContentPart\n662\t\n663\t\tfor _, block := range content {\n664\t\t\tswitch block.Type {\n665\t\t\tcase \"text\":\n666\t\t\t\tparts = append(parts, models.OpenAIContentPart{\n667\t\t\t\t\tType: \"text\",\n668\t\t\t\t\tText: block.Text,\n669\t\t\t\t})\n670\t\t\tcase \"image\":\n671\t\t\t\tif block.Source != nil {\n672\t\t\t\t\tdataURL := fmt.Sprintf(\"data:%s;base64,%s\", block.Source.MediaType, block.Source.Data)\n673\t\t\t\t\tparts = append(parts, models.OpenAIContentPart{\n674\t\t\t\t\t\tType: \"image_url\",\n675\t\t\t\t\t\tImageURL: &models.ImageURL{\n676\t\t\t\t\t\t\tURL: dataURL,\n677\t\t\t\t\t\t},\n678\t\t\t\t\t})\n679\t\t\t\t}\n680\t\t\t}\n681\t\t\t// Skip tool_result blocks - handled separately\n682\t\t}\n683\t\n684\t\t// If only text content, use string format\n685\t\tif len(parts) == 1 && parts[0].Type == \"text\" {\n686\t\t\treturn models.OpenAIMessage{\n687\t\t\t\tRole:    \"user\",\n688\t\t\t\tContent: parts[0].Text,\n689\t\t\t}\n690\t\t}\n691\t\n692\t\t// Otherwise use array format\n693\t\tif len(parts) == 0 {\n694\t\t\treturn models.OpenAIMessage{\n695\t\t\t\tRole:    \"user\",\n696\t\t\t\tContent: \"\",\n697\t\t\t}\n698\t\t}\n699\t\n700\t\treturn models.OpenAIMessage{\n701\t\t\tRole:    \"user\",\n702\t\t\tContent: contentPartsToInterface(parts),\n703\t\t}\n704\t}\n705\t\n706\t// contentPartsToInterface converts content parts to interface for JSON marshaling.\n707\tfunc contentPartsToInterface(parts []models.OpenAIContentPart) interface{} {\n708\t\tresult := make([]interface{}, len(parts))\n709\t\tfor i, p := range parts {\n710\t\t\tresult[i] = p\n711\t\t}\n712\t\treturn result\n713\t}\n714\t\n715\t// extractToolResults extracts tool result blocks and converts to OpenAI tool messages.\n716\tfunc extractToolResults(content []models.ContentBlock) []models.OpenAIMessage {\n717\t\tvar results []models.OpenAIMessage\n718\t\n719\t\tfor _, block := range content {\n720\t\t\tif block.Type == \"tool_result\" {\n721\t\t\t\t// Extract content from the tool result (can be string or array)\n722\t\t\t\toutput := extractToolResultContentForChat(block)\n723\t\n724\t\t\t\t// If the tool result indicates an error, prefix the output\n725\t\t\t\tif block.IsError {\n726\t\t\t\t\toutput = \"[Error] \" + output\n727\t\t\t\t}\n728\t\n729\t\t\t\ttoolMsg := models.OpenAIMessage{\n730\t\t\t\t\tRole:       \"tool\",\n731\t\t\t\t\tContent:    output,\n732\t\t\t\t\tToolCallID: block.ToolUseID,\n733\t\t\t\t}\n734\t\t\t\tresults = append(results, toolMsg)\n735\t\t\t}\n736\t\t}\n737\t\n738\t\treturn results\n739\t}\n740\t\n741\t// extractToolResultContentForChat extracts content from a tool result block for Chat Completions API.\n742\t// The content can be a string or an array of content blocks.\n743\tfunc extractToolResultContentForChat(block models.ContentBlock) string {\n744\t\tif block.Content == nil {\n745\t\t\treturn \"\"\n746\t\t}\n747\t\n748\t\t// Try as string first (most common case)\n749\t\tif str, ok := block.Content.(string); ok {\n750\t\t\treturn str\n751\t\t}\n752\t\n753\t\t// Try as array of content blocks\n754\t\tif arr, ok := block.Content.([]interface{}); ok {\n755\t\t\tvar parts []string\n756\t\t\tfor _, item := range arr {\n757\t\t\t\tif itemMap, ok := item.(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n758\t\t\t\t\t// Extract text from nested content blocks\n759\t\t\t\t\tif itemType, ok := itemMap[\"type\"].(string); ok && itemType == \"text\" {\n760\t\t\t\t\t\tif text, ok := itemMap[\"text\"].(string); ok {\n761\t\t\t\t\t\t\tparts = append(parts, text)\n762\t\t\t\t\t\t}\n763\t\t\t\t\t}\n764\t\t\t\t}\n765\t\t\t}\n766\t\t\treturn strings.Join(parts, \"\\n\")\n767\t\t}\n768\t\n769\t\t// Fallback: marshal to JSON\n770\t\tdata, err := json.Marshal(block.Content)\n771\t\tif err != nil {\n772\t\t\treturn \"\"\n773\t\t}\n774\t\treturn string(data)\n775\t}\n776\t\n777\t// transformAssistantMessage transforms assistant message content to OpenAI format.\n778\tfunc transformAssistantMessage(content []models.ContentBlock) models.OpenAIMessage {\n779\t\tmsg := models.OpenAIMessage{\n780\t\t\tRole: \"assistant\",\n781\t\t}\n782\t\n783\t\tvar textParts []string\n784\t\tvar toolCalls []models.OpenAIToolCall\n785\t\n786\t\tfor i, block := range content {\n787\t\t\tswitch block.Type {\n788\t\t\tcase \"text\":\n789\t\t\t\ttextParts = append(textParts, block.Text)\n790\t\t\tcase \"tool_use\":\n791\t\t\t\tinputJSON, _ := json.Marshal(block.Input)\n792\t\t\t\ttoolCalls = append(toolCalls, models.OpenAIToolCall{\n793\t\t\t\t\tID:    block.ID,\n794\t\t\t\t\tType:  \"function\",\n795\t\t\t\t\tIndex: i,\n796\t\t\t\t\tFunction: models.OpenAIFunctionCall{\n797\t\t\t\t\t\tName:      block.Name,\n798\t\t\t\t\t\tArguments: string(inputJSON),\n799\t\t\t\t\t},\n800\t\t\t\t})\n801\t\t\t}\n802\t\t}\n803\t\n804\t\tif len(textParts) > 0 {\n805\t\t\tmsg.Content = strings.Join(textParts, \"\")\n806\t\t}\n807\t\n808\t\tif len(toolCalls) > 0 {\n809\t\t\tmsg.ToolCalls = toolCalls\n810\t\t}\n811\t\n812\t\treturn msg\n813\t}\n814\t\n815\t// getTextContent extracts text content from content blocks.\n816\tfunc getTextContent(content []models.ContentBlock) string {\n817\t\tvar parts []string\n818\t\tfor _, block := range content {\n819\t\t\tif block.Type == \"text\" {\n820\t\t\t\tparts = append(parts, block.Text)\n821\t\t\t}\n822\t\t}\n823\t\treturn strings.Join(parts, \"\")\n824\t}\n825\t\n826\t// transformTools converts Anthropic tools to OpenAI format.\n827\t// IMPORTANT: We set Strict=false because Anthropic's tool schemas mark ALL parameters as\n828\t// required, but Claude Code only provides values for truly required parameters. With strict\n829\t// mode enabled, OpenAI rejects tool calls when optional parameters are missing.\n830\tfunc transformTools(tools []models.AnthropicTool) []models.OpenAITool {\n831\t\tresult := make([]models.OpenAITool, len(tools))\n832\t\n833\t\tfor i, tool := range tools {\n834\t\t\t// Handle computer use tools (have a \"type\" field like \"computer_20241024\")\n835\t\t\ttoolName := tool.Name\n836\t\t\ttoolDescription := tool.Description\n837\t\t\ttoolParams := tool.InputSchema\n838\t\n839\t\t\tif isComputerUseTool(tool.Type) {\n840\t\t\t\t// Transform computer use tool to generic function\n841\t\t\t\ttoolName, toolDescription, toolParams = transformComputerUseTool(tool)\n842\t\t\t} else if IsClaudeCodeTool(tool.Name) {\n843\t\t\t\t// Transform Claude Code tools to ensure proper schema for all providers\n844\t\t\t\ttoolName, toolDescription, toolParams = GetClaudeCodeToolDefinition(tool)\n845\t\t\t}\n846\t\n847\t\t\t// Clean up input schema (remove format: \"uri\" which OpenAI doesn't support)\n848\t\t\t// AND fix required array to only include truly required parameters\n849\t\t\tparams := cleanupSchemaForChatCompletions(toolParams)\n850\t\n851\t\t\tresult[i] = models.OpenAITool{\n852\t\t\t\tType: \"function\",\n853\t\t\t\tFunction: models.OpenAIFunction{\n854\t\t\t\t\tName:        toolName,\n855\t\t\t\t\tDescription: toolDescription,\n856\t\t\t\t\tParameters:  params,\n857\t\t\t\t\tStrict:      false, // CRITICAL: Don't use strict mode - Anthropic marks all params as required\n858\t\t\t\t},\n859\t\t\t}\n860\t\t}\n861\t\n862\t\treturn result\n863\t}\n864\t\n865\t// isComputerUseTool checks if the tool type is a computer use tool.\n866\tfunc isComputerUseTool(toolType string) bool {\n867\t\tswitch toolType {\n868\t\tcase models.ToolTypeComputer, models.ToolTypeTextEditor, models.ToolTypeBash:\n869\t\t\treturn true\n870\t\tdefault:\n871\t\t\treturn false\n872\t\t}\n873\t}\n874\t\n875\t// transformComputerUseTool transforms Anthropic computer use tools to generic function format.\n876\t// This allows proxying computer use workflows through OpenAI-compatible endpoints.\n877\tfunc transformComputerUseTool(tool models.AnthropicTool) (name, description string, params interface{}) {\n878\t\tswitch tool.Type {\n879\t\tcase models.ToolTypeComputer:\n880\t\t\t// Computer tool: screen capture, mouse, keyboard actions\n881\t\t\treturn \"computer\",\n882\t\t\t\t\"Control the computer - take screenshots, move mouse, click, type text, and execute keyboard shortcuts\",\n883\t\t\t\tmap[string]interface{}{\n884\t\t\t\t\t\"type\": \"object\",\n885\t\t\t\t\t\"properties\": map[string]interface{}{\n886\t\t\t\t\t\t\"action\": map[string]interface{}{\n887\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\"type\":        \"string\",\n888\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\"enum\":        []string{\"screenshot\", \"mouse_move\", \"left_click\", \"right_click\", \"double_click\", \"middle_click\", \"left_click_drag\", \"type\", \"key\", \"scroll\"},\n889\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\"description\": \"The action to perform\",\n890\t\t\t\t\t\t},\n891\t\t\t\t\t\t\"coordinate\": map[string]interface{}{\n892\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\"type\":        \"array\",\n893\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\"items\":       map[string]interface{}{\"type\": \"integer\"},\n894\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\"description\": \"Screen coordinates [x, y] for mouse actions\",\n895\t\t\t\t\t\t},\n896\t\t\t\t\t\t\"text\": map[string]interface{}{\n897\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\"type\":        \"string\",\n898\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\"description\": \"Text to type (for 'type' action) or key combination (for 'key' action)\",\n899\t\t\t\t\t\t},\n900\t\t\t\t\t},\n901\t\t\t\t\t\"required\": []string{\"action\"},\n902\t\t\t\t}\n903\t\n904\t\tcase models.ToolTypeTextEditor:\n905\t\t\t// Text editor tool: view, edit, create files\n906\t\t\treturn \"str_replace_editor\",\n907\t\t\t\t\"View, create, and edit files using a text editor. Supports viewing file contents, creating new files, and making precise text replacements.\",\n908\t\t\t\tmap[string]interface{}{\n909\t\t\t\t\t\"type\": \"object\",\n910\t\t\t\t\t\"properties\": map[string]interface{}{\n911\t\t\t\t\t\t\"command\": map[string]interface{}{\n912\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\"type\":        \"string\",\n913\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\"enum\":        []string{\"view\", \"create\", \"str_replace\", \"insert\", \"undo_edit\"},\n914\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\"description\": \"The editor command to execute\",\n915\t\t\t\t\t\t},\n916\t\t\t\t\t\t\"path\": map[string]interface{}{\n917\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\"type\":        \"string\",\n918\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\"description\": \"File path to operate on\",\n919\t\t\t\t\t\t},\n920\t\t\t\t\t\t\"file_text\": map[string]interface{}{\n921\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\"type\":        \"string\",\n922\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\"description\": \"File content for 'create' command\",\n923\t\t\t\t\t\t},\n924\t\t\t\t\t\t\"old_str\": map[string]interface{}{\n925\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\"type\":        \"string\",\n926\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\"description\": \"String to find for 'str_replace' command\",\n927\t\t\t\t\t\t},\n928\t\t\t\t\t\t\"new_str\": map[string]interface{}{\n929\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\"type\":        \"string\",\n930\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\"description\": \"Replacement string for 'str_replace' command\",\n931\t\t\t\t\t\t},\n932\t\t\t\t\t\t\"insert_line\": map[string]interface{}{\n933\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\"type\":        \"integer\",\n934\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\"description\": \"Line number for 'insert' command\",\n935\t\t\t\t\t\t},\n936\t\t\t\t\t\t\"view_range\": map[string]interface{}{\n937\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\"type\":        \"array\",\n938\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\"items\":       map[string]interface{}{\"type\": \"integer\"},\n939\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\"description\": \"Line range [start, end] for 'view' command\",\n940\t\t\t\t\t\t},\n941\t\t\t\t\t},\n942\t\t\t\t\t\"required\": []string{\"command\", \"path\"},\n943\t\t\t\t}\n944\t\n945\t\tcase models.ToolTypeBash:\n946\t\t\t// Bash tool: execute shell commands\n947\t\t\treturn \"bash\",\n948\t\t\t\t\"Execute bash shell commands on the system. Use for running scripts, system administration, file operations, and other command-line tasks.\",\n949\t\t\t\tmap[string]interface{}{\n950\t\t\t\t\t\"type\": \"object\",\n951\t\t\t\t\t\"properties\": map[string]interface{}{\n952\t\t\t\t\t\t\"command\": map[string]interface{}{\n953\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\"type\":        \"string\",\n954\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\"description\": \"The bash command to execute\",\n955\t\t\t\t\t\t},\n956\t\t\t\t\t\t\"restart\": map[string]interface{}{\n957\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\"type\":        \"boolean\",\n958\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\"description\": \"Whether to restart the bash session before executing\",\n959\t\t\t\t\t\t},\n960\t\t\t\t\t},\n961\t\t\t\t\t\"required\": []string{\"command\"},\n962\t\t\t\t}\n963\t\n964\t\tdefault:\n965\t\t\t// Unknown tool type, pass through as-is\n966\t\t\treturn tool.Name, tool.Description, tool.InputSchema\n967\t\t}\n968\t}\n969\t\n970\t// cleanupSchema removes unsupported schema properties.\n971\tfunc cleanupSchema(schema interface{}) interface{} {\n972\t\tif schema == nil {\n973\t\t\treturn nil\n974\t\t}\n975\t\n976\t\tdata, err := json.Marshal(schema)\n977\t\tif err != nil {\n978\t\t\treturn schema\n979\t\t}\n980\t\n981\t\tvar schemaMap map[string]interface{}\n982\t\tif err := json.Unmarshal(data, &schemaMap); err != nil {\n983\t\t\treturn schema\n984\t\t}\n985\t\n986\t\tcleanupSchemaMap(schemaMap)\n987\t\treturn schemaMap\n988\t}\n989\t\n990\t// cleanupSchemaMap recursively cleans up schema properties.\n991\tfunc cleanupSchemaMap(schema map[string]interface{}) {\n992\t\t// Remove unsupported format types\n993\t\tif format, ok := schema[\"format\"].(string); ok {\n994\t\t\tif format == \"uri\" {\n995\t\t\t\tdelete(schema, \"format\")\n996\t\t\t}\n997\t\t}\n998\t\n999\t\t// Recurse into properties\n1000\t\tif props, ok := schema[\"properties\"].(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n1001\t\t\tfor _, v := range props {\n1002\t\t\t\tif propMap, ok := v.(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n1003\t\t\t\t\tcleanupSchemaMap(propMap)\n1004\t\t\t\t}\n1005\t\t\t}\n1006\t\t}\n1007\t\n1008\t\t// Recurse into items (for arrays)\n1009\t\tif items, ok := schema[\"items\"].(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n1010\t\t\tcleanupSchemaMap(items)\n1011\t\t}\n1012\t}\n1013\t\n1014\t// cleanupSchemaForChatCompletions prepares an Anthropic tool schema for the Chat Completions API.\n1015\t// This includes removing unsupported format types and fixing the required array\n1016\t// to only include truly required parameters.\n1017\tfunc cleanupSchemaForChatCompletions(schema interface{}) interface{} {\n1018\t\tif schema == nil {\n1019\t\t\treturn nil\n1020\t\t}\n1021\t\n1022\t\tdata, err := json.Marshal(schema)\n1023\t\tif err != nil {\n1024\t\t\treturn schema\n1025\t\t}\n1026\t\n1027\t\tvar schemaMap map[string]interface{}\n1028\t\tif err := json.Unmarshal(data, &schemaMap); err != nil {\n1029\t\t\treturn schema\n1030\t\t}\n1031\t\n1032\t\t// Clean up the schema including required array filtering\n1033\t\tcleanupSchemaMapForChatCompletions(schemaMap)\n1034\t\treturn schemaMap\n1035\t}\n1036\t\n1037\t// cleanupSchemaMapForChatCompletions recursively cleans up schema properties for Chat Completions API.\n1038\t// Key fix: Only include truly required parameters (those without defaults and not nullable).\n1039\tfunc cleanupSchemaMapForChatCompletions(schema map[string]interface{}) {\n1040\t\t// Remove unsupported format types\n1041\t\tif format, ok := schema[\"format\"].(string); ok {\n1042\t\t\tif format == \"uri\" {\n1043\t\t\t\tdelete(schema, \"format\")\n1044\t\t\t}\n1045\t\t}\n1046\t\n1047\t\t// CRITICAL: Remove any \"strict\" field from the schema itself\n1048\t\t// Claude Code may include \"strict\": true in tool definitions, which causes\n1049\t\t// validation failures when optional parameters are missing.\n1050\t\tdelete(schema, \"strict\")\n1051\t\n1052\t\t// Process properties and fix required array\n1053\t\tif props, ok := schema[\"properties\"].(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n1054\t\t\t// Identify truly required parameters\n1055\t\t\ttrulyRequired := identifyTrulyRequiredForChat(props, schema)\n1056\t\t\tif len(trulyRequired) > 0 {\n1057\t\t\t\tschema[\"required\"] = trulyRequired\n1058\t\t\t} else {\n1059\t\t\t\t// If no truly required params, remove required array entirely\n1060\t\t\t\tdelete(schema, \"required\")\n1061\t\t\t}\n1062\t\n1063\t\t\t// Recurse into properties\n1064\t\t\tfor _, v := range props {\n1065\t\t\t\tif propMap, ok := v.(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n1066\t\t\t\t\tcleanupSchemaMapForChatCompletions(propMap)\n1067\t\t\t\t}\n1068\t\t\t}\n1069\t\t}\n1070\t\n1071\t\t// Recurse into items (for arrays)\n1072\t\tif items, ok := schema[\"items\"].(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n1073\t\t\tcleanupSchemaMapForChatCompletions(items)\n1074\t\t}\n1075\t}\n1076\t\n1077\t// identifyTrulyRequiredForChat determines which parameters are truly required.\n1078\t// A parameter is truly required if:\n1079\t// 1. It appears in the original required array\n1080\t// 2. It doesn't have a default value\n1081\t// 3. It's not nullable\n1082\t// 4. It doesn't have a description containing optional-indicating phrases\n1083\t// 5. It's not a boolean type (booleans are almost always optional flags)\n1084\tfunc identifyTrulyRequiredForChat(props, schema map[string]interface{}) []string {\n1085\t\t// Pre-allocate with estimated capacity\n1086\t\ttrulyRequired := make([]string, 0, len(props)/2)\n1087\t\n1088\t\t// Get original required array\n1089\t\toriginalRequired := make(map[string]bool)\n1090\t\tif req, ok := schema[\"required\"].([]interface{}); ok {\n1091\t\t\tfor _, r := range req {\n1092\t\t\t\tif s, ok := r.(string); ok {\n1093\t\t\t\t\toriginalRequired[s] =
